=== SIMD vs Scalar Performance Comparison ===

Comparing SIMD-accelerated vector ops vs pure scalar loops.
Speedup = Scalar Time / SIMD Time

--- ADD (int32, 4096 elements) ---
  SIMD (checked_add_vec):  438.240 ns
  Scalar loop:             7.767 us
  Speedup: 17.72x

--- SUB (int32, 4096 elements) ---
  SIMD (checked_sub_vec):  466.200 ns
  Scalar loop:             8.325 us
  Speedup: 17.86x

--- MUL (int32, 4096 elements) ---
  SIMD (checked_mul_vec):  932.500 ns
  Scalar loop:             7.833 us
  Speedup: 8.40x

--- Per-Element Timing ---
  Add: 0.107 ns/element (SIMD)
  Sub: 0.114 ns/element (SIMD)
  Mul: 0.228 ns/element (SIMD)

--- Architecture Info ---
  Backend: SSE2
  int32 width: 4 lanes
  int64 width: 1 lanes
  Saturating HW: no

Raw vector add loop:
  [2025-12-28 01:25:25] CPU: 3169 MHz (14% throttled)
  Average time per operation: 73.610 ns
  Total for 10000 iterations: 736.100 us
checked_add_vec:
  [2025-12-28 01:25:25] CPU: 3169 MHz (14% throttled)
  Average time per operation: 122.600 ns
  Total for 10000 iterations: 1.226 ms

--- Interpretation ---

1. Scalar operations: Checked adds ~1-5ns overhead for safety.

2. Multiplication/Division: Higher overhead due to complex overflow checks.

3. Vector operations: SIMD amortizes per-element checking cost.

4. Use SaturatingPolicy for lowest overhead when errors are recoverable.

PERFORMANCE BENCHMARKS - JsonLite vs FatPJson
==========================================================

Small JSON Parsing (<1KB):

  JsonLite:      927.510 ns
  FatPJson:  912.080 ns
  Ratio:         0.98x

Large Array Parsing (10000 elements):

  JsonLite:      1.205 ms
  FatPJson:  1.088 ms
  Ratio:         0.90x

Array Operations (SmallVector vs vector):

Warning: Total measurement (0.0256 ms) may have insufficient precision. Consider increasing iterations.
  JsonArray (std::vector):      161.600 ns
  FatPJsonArray (SmallVector):  25.600 ns
  Speedup:                      6.31x faster

Object Operations (FlatMap vs map):

  JsonObject (std::map):   1.276 us
  FatPJsonObject (FlatMap): 1.090 us
  Speedup:                 1.17x faster

StringPool Memory Savings:

  Standard memory (1000 objects): 16000 bytes
  Pooled memory (unique keys):    16 bytes
  Memory savings:                 99.9%

Error Handling (Expected vs exceptions):

  Exception-based (JsonLite): 3.565 us
  Expected-based (FatPJson):  4.152 us
  Speedup on error path:      0.9x faster

Memory-Mapped I/O vs Regular I/O:

  Regular I/O:  7.844 ms
  Memory-mapped: 6.282 ms
  Speedup:      1.25x faster

=== HASH QUALITY IMPACT (StableHashMap: std::hash vs SplitMix64) ===
Compares StableHashMap<K,V> vs StableHashMap<K,V,SplitMix64Hash>.
SplitMix64: high-quality 64-bit mixer (not borrowed from any library).
SHM(std) = StableHashMap with std::hash, SHM(SM64) = StableHashMap with SplitMix64.

  [2025-12-28 01:28:03] CPU: 2506 MHz (32% throttled)
   10000 | Hit: SHM(std)=  30.05 SHM(SM64)=   6.64 (SM64 wins) | Miss: SHM(std)=  20.04 SHM(SM64)=   4.83 (SM64 wins)
  [2025-12-28 01:28:03] CPU: 3096 MHz (16% throttled)
  100000 | Hit: SHM(std)=  36.09 SHM(SM64)=  10.08 (SM64 wins) | Miss: SHM(std)=  23.64 SHM(SM64)=   6.70 (SM64 wins)
  [2025-12-28 01:28:04] CPU: 3059 MHz (17% throttled)
  500000 | Hit: SHM(std)=  67.94 SHM(SM64)=  26.51 (SM64 wins) | Miss: SHM(std)=  21.68 SHM(SM64)=   6.10 (SM64 wins)
  [2025-12-28 01:28:05] CPU: 3501 MHz (5% throttled)
 1000000 | Hit: SHM(std)= 126.98 SHM(SM64)=  35.93 (SM64 wins) | Miss: SHM(std)=  29.79 SHM(SM64)=   7.29 (SM64 wins)

=== STRING HETEROGENEOUS LOOKUP ===
Benefit of find(string_view) vs find(temp std::string).
Real workloads often have string keys but view-based lookups.

    1000 | string_view=  39.78 ns | temp_string=  91.00 ns | Speedup: 2.29x (saves 51.2 ns)
   10000 | string_view=   48.8 ns | temp_string=  121.5 ns | Speedup: 2.49x (saves 72.7 ns)
  100000 | string_view=   64.2 ns | temp_string=   99.8 ns | Speedup: 1.55x (saves 35.6 ns)

Key Findings:
  1. Integer fast path matches std::to_string
  2. Boolean fast path ~10ns (was ~470ns)
  3. Float fast path matches std::to_string
  4. String passthrough is near-zero cost
  5. Pair/tuple optimized with string concat
  6. toStringConcat uses fold expression (no ostringstream)
  7. Container cost scales linearly with element count

--- Interpretation ---

1. Unchecked StrongId matches raw int exactly - confirms zero wrapper overhead.

2. Checked multiplication ~2x slower - this is the cost of overflow detection,
   not wrapper overhead. Use UncheckedOpPolicy if profiling shows this matters.

3. All other checked operations match raw int - overflow checks are
   optimized away or have negligible cost for add/increment.
